### Identity (reviewed)

Interactive native CPC star-field demo by Diego Freniche, with separate entity lifecycle, star generation, directional physics and rendering systems; includes keyboard direction and hyperspace effects.
- https://github.com/dfreniche/stars/commit/7acd22c0e5736bd33b57d6f76ecf05812a8385dc

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected main loop plus concrete star position, destruction and screen-byte rendering routines; substantive small demo rather than only a toolchain hello-world. README declares MIT; no standalone MIT text found. Build template contains LGPL-3.0-or-later notice, so preserve component distinctions.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable ZX Spectrum 48K workbench containing its own machine-cycle Z80 core/disassembler, Spectrum memory and ULA/tape/audio model, source-level debugger, macro assembler and desktop/CLI frontends.

Read CPU interrupt/HALT stepping and fixpoint assembler implementation. Project labels status early; published conformance counts are author claims, not independently verified. ### Identity (reviewed)

Host-side tape/snapshot conversion and playback tool paired with a native Z80 turbo-loader for real Spectrum 48K/128K machines. Handles TAP/TZX/snapshots, pulse generation, WAV output, compression and optional Qt UI.
- https://github.com/oxidaan/zqloader/commit/77bdbe94e35f60f22b0ea179440ae2c1f390934f

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected actual pulse/loader orchestration, WAV RIFF header writing and 889-line Z80 loader. Pinned latest change fixes WAV output channel volume handling. MIT in LICENSE.txt and source headers; vendored miniaudio retains its own component licensing.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable batch and Z80 loader pipeline that extracts game blocks, rearranges/crops SCREEN$ data, compresses with ZX0, and packages replacement compressed TAP/TZX or disk loaders. Per-game configurations and multilevel disk loaders are included.
- https://github.com/0sAND1s/SpectrumGameCompressor/commit/83810a88a79a8d5c30da7d3525277a2add8874da

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Z80 assembly sequel for CPC 464 by Daniel Saura Martínez and Enrique Vidal Cayuela. Contains modular game/menu/entity managers, collision and physics systems, power-ups, sprites, level maps and Arkos music assets.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original 2019 native CPC game by Enrique Vidal Cayuela and Daniel Saura Martínez, with Z80 player/entity/enemy logic, keyboard/game state loop, sprite animation and two dozen Tiled maps.

Native CPC 464 game created for an Automatic Reasoning course and CPCRetroDev 2016 by Cristina Rivera, Miguel Sancho and Fernando Verdejo, with map-based movement, enemies, knives, scoring and custom pathfinding.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native BBC Micro four-player Riichi Mahjong implementation in one substantial BeebAsm source, with Mode 7 tile display, human controls, computer opponents, scoring and practice hints.
- https://github.com/danj2k/mahjong/commit/7db772f117209f1dc28cee159161c435a3f73207

Read 6,562-line source and known-issues report. Do not repeat README completeness claims: docs explicitly say weak opponent hand-building, incomplete chombo, missing triple-ron and Tenhou/Chiihou detection, and active debug traps. ### Identity (reviewed)

Stephen Scott’s remaster of his 1993 BBC Micro arcade game, retaining BBC BASIC source while upgrading from MODE 5 to MODE 2, graphics/sound, bugs and late-level difficulty. Includes a BeebAsm disk-packaging script.

### Identity (reviewed)

Long-running native 6502 Scorched Earth-style multiplayer artillery game by Tomasz Pecko and Pawel Kalinowski, with deformable terrain, weapon shop, computer opponents, POKEY audio and Atari 5200 support plus a work-in-progress C64 port.

### Identity (reviewed)

Three standalone modern Python tools recover BBC BASIC V text, PackDir archives, and ADFS floppy images from Acorn RISC OS media. Implementation handles inline ARM assembler tokens, line-number references, LZW, metadata and load/execute address preservation.
- https://github.com/merlinorg/risc-os/blob/43a8c16e6921d62407a502f9cdfe15e23924b3f3/unpackdir.py

### Identity (reviewed)

Reverse-engineered specification, Python parser/applier and encoder for K. Higashide’s 1992 BdifSet .bfd binary patch format. Recovered from disassembly of Human68k 68000 binaries, with bidirectional delta operations and CRC handling implemented.
- https://github.com/famicomical/x68k-bdif/blob/cf90f3216ae3d5e5b9da810696b61adbaf3bc9dd/bfd.py
- https://github.com/famicomical/x68k-bdif/blob/cf90f3216ae3d5e5b9da810696b61adbaf3bc9dd/mkbfd.py

### Identity (reviewed)

X68000 floppy formatter lineage: original personal utility preserved in fdformat and redesigned FDFORMAT2 implements parallel per-drive state machines, physical formatting, BPB/FAT/root-directory/minimal-IPL logical formatting and sector sliding. Represented as one project lineage.
- https://github.com/shimase68000/fdformat2/blob/348dfb0f1e8cbadf42391e29a330322d00778722/proj/src/physical_format.s

### Identity (reviewed)

Python decryption and patching implementation for early Macintosh PACE-protected software, explaining on-the-fly self-modifying decryption, bad-block floppy checks and debugger/timing checks. Multiple per-title scripts recover inspectable 68000 program bytes and alter protection checks.
- https://github.com/barberd/depace/blob/9b8d504a133fa0b2db38de403d0a3c34bcf3c009/decrypt/SwordOfKadash-decrypt.py

### Identity (reviewed)

Compact native Macintosh boot disk driver implemented in C with m68k assembly entry/header shim. Implements Apple partition scanning, registering/mounting HFS volumes, SCSI READ(10)/chunked WRITE(10), DriveStatus and drive icon in a position-independent raw driver.
- https://github.com/ingpaschke/miniscsi/blob/93bd4bf277f957e1de0e57357631cdfd3255ef38/body.c
- https://github.com/ingpaschke/miniscsi/blob/93bd4bf277f957e1de0e57357631cdfd3255ef38/head.s

- https://github.com/ingpaschke/miniscsi/commit/93bd4bf277f957e1de0e57357631cdfd3255ef38

### Classification (reviewed)

README says QEMU q800 tested, not real hardware, but latest commit claims successful LC475/BlueSCSI hardware boot; preserve this upstream discrepancy. MIT.

### Identity (reviewed)

Clean-room C++20/raylib reconstruction of Lander behavior using Mark Moxon’s annotated Archimedes ARM analysis as specification. Implements terrain, ship dynamics, mouse polar control, rendering and collision rather than executing an embedded original CPU.

### Identity (reviewed)

Game-specific SNES disassembly in Yoshifanatic1’s ROM framework, with its own bank/routine macros, native memory maps, SPC audio engine and original-ROM asset extraction/reassembly workflow. CPU reset/main/NMI routines and explicit compressed SPC engine are present; only USA supported despite generic multi-region menu entries.
- https://github.com/Yoshifanatic1/SimCity-SNES-Disassembly/commit/a6cc787f80c7d05719b3ab175be8159a5e606731

### Identity (reviewed)

Game-specific SNES disassembly in Yoshifanatic1’s ROM framework, with its own bank/routine macros, native memory maps, SPC audio engine and original-ROM asset extraction/reassembly workflow. Read native reset, lives/level-state initialization and bank reconstruction. USA is the documented supported release.

### Identity (reviewed)

Game-specific SNES disassembly in Yoshifanatic1’s ROM framework, with its own bank/routine macros, native memory maps, SPC audio engine and original-ROM asset extraction/reassembly workflow. Large bank/routine source, text tables and decoded SPC engine are present. Only USA map/extraction supported; JP/EU/French/German assembly menu options are framework scaffolding, not evidence of completed support.

Standalone shared tooling extracted from Pokémon disassemblies, with RGBDS map parsing/free-space analysis, source/symbol utilities and 1bpp/2bpp-to-PNG asset conversion.

### Identity (reviewed)

Matching decompilation of original PlayStation SDK libraries, with substantive reconstructed sound sequencing, SPU and compression functions, version-conditional implementations and object/library comparison infrastructure.
- https://github.com/sozud/psy-q-decomp/commit/6edf9b24721ba02a53eb423b8a7497b82ba0768c

### Identity (reviewed)

Ryan C. Gordon’s independent OS/2 compatibility implementation loads OS/2 NE/LX executables and maps their APIs to Linux libraries. Pinned lx_loader.c validates modules and native/doscalls.c implements OS/2 calls; this is substantive reusable compatibility software.
- https://github.com/icculus/2ine/blob/1ca194f39b6322d5c6cc1006616ed6a62f74c193/README.md
- https://github.com/icculus/2ine/blob/1ca194f39b6322d5c6cc1006616ed6a62f74c193/lx_loader.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable Python tools for Windows 3.1 binary research: derive NE segment maps from DOSBox memory, convert CPU coverage into IDA/Lighthouse addresses, and reconstruct OPTLOADER-compressed executables. optloader.py contains an explicit modeled loader and decompression logic.
- https://github.com/moralrecordings/win16-debug/blob/d4aef518fb22991d1d22f76727dfaa08e5d5b370/README.rst
- https://github.com/moralrecordings/win16-debug/blob/d4aef518fb22991d1d22f76727dfaa08e5d5b370/get_segtable.py
- https://github.com/moralrecordings/win16-debug/blob/d4aef518fb22991d1d22f76727dfaa08e5d5b370/optloader.py

- https://github.com/moralrecordings/win16-debug/blob/d4aef518fb22991d1d22f76727dfaa08e5d5b370/LICENSE
- https://github.com/moralrecordings/win16-debug/blob/d4aef518fb22991d1d22f76727dfaa08e5d5b370/LICENSE_OPTLINK
- https://github.com/moralrecordings/win16-debug/commit/d4aef518fb22991d1d22f76727dfaa08e5d5b370

### Classification (reviewed)

Not an emulator wrapper. Reusable memory/coverage conversion and executable unpacking; developed for Director/Projector but not confined to one game. BSD-3-Clause for project; optloader.py derives from DigitalMars OPTLINK under Boost Software License 1.0.

### Identity (reviewed)

Japheth’s source collection of DOSAlloc, DosMonW, GDIStat, Notify, SetMode and Share. Inspected implementation reads USER/GDI heap status and hooks DOS calls, with C++ UI and 16-bit assembly sources.
- https://github.com/Baron-von-Riedesel/Win16Tools/blob/904dd2d5d9c19abc0070b09bc60c36b3c4d0295b/Readme.txt

### Classification (reviewed)

Concrete diagnostics suite, not just example stubs. DOSAlloc/SetMode/Share specifically target Windows 3.1 running with HX DOSX replacement and HDPMI16. No repository-wide license file or explicit grant located in inspected tree/README; source available, reuse rights unestablished.

### Identity (reviewed)

Josh Patterson’s native retro-developed Windows 3.11 HTTP server. C++ Winsock implementation binds/listens from XML configuration, with a custom segmented-memory manager and static-file server UI.

### Identity (reviewed)

Aaron Ardiri and Michael Ethetton's Liberty 1.3.1 source archive preserves a reusable original Game Boy emulator written for Palm handhelds, including 68k CPU/video/sound code and a ROM-to-PDB converter.

Hilary Cheng and David Schmenk's Apple II/IIe emulator for Palm PDAs combines a substantial hand-written 68k 6502 interpreter, memory-mapped Apple I/O, video and disk-image conversion tools.

### Identity (reviewed)

The implemented portion of Patrick Barron's planned UNIVAC 1100 emulator is a usable C image-inspection toolkit. uimage2strings unpacks paired 36-bit words from disk-pack images and decodes 9-bit ASCII or 6-bit FIELDATA for external analysis.
- https://github.com/patbarron/u1100/blob/c758245a3d58fa6bfd2bcd3c2ef30bdecd7233bf/tools/uimage2strings/uimage2strings.c

A reusable TypeScript PDP-1 interpreter with 18-bit memory/registers, instruction groups, console switches, paper-tape loading and vector display, presented through an Angular front panel.

### Identity (reviewed)

Paul Kimpel's detailed General Precision LGP-21 implementation reconstructs the processor, rotating fixed-head disk memory, arithmetic mechanisms, control panel, Flexowriter and Tally paper-tape devices from machine manuals.

### Identity (reviewed)

Kenneth Gober's C# SEL 810 suite includes a substantial processor/memory/console model, TCP peripheral links, tape server, BASIC tape construction, loader-aware tape dumper and control-flow-assisted disassembler.

### Identity (reviewed)

Historical TRIPOS BCPL operating-system and tool sources shared with permission from Tim King, covering the shell and utilities plus Nova, PDP-11, LSI4 and Motorola 68000 assembly/compiler support. This is the historical lineage underlying early AmigaDOS, not an AmigaDOS replacement.
- https://github.com/borb/tripos/blob/38820c9c9f9db6b41a6b2ae24b430da679df6572/com/shell

### Identity (reviewed)

Peter Onon's Elliott 803 emulator models 39-bit arithmetic and processor operations alongside an unusually physical operator experience: charger, contactor, hours-run counter, sound and console wiring.

### Identity (reviewed)

Andrew Herbert's maintained extension of Lee Wittenberg's EDSAC emulator implements machine orders/arithmetic and tape-style I/O, adding tracing, binary store upload and execution limits for the EDSAC Replica Project.
- https://github.com/andrewjherbert/EDSAC-Emulator/blob/0c4e6d6d3ad55cc64f0d7ca8b85fd0ae857dee46/exec_ord.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Josh Dersch's independently written Python implementation of a standard 4K-word PDP-8 includes instruction dispatch, memory, console Teletype and paper-tape loading; it is broader than a single program wrapper.
- https://github.com/jdersch/PDP-8/blob/d77bf75c164b904f3f60cff096e6b9b9e3524acd/pdp8.py
- https://github.com/jdersch/PDP-8/blob/d77bf75c164b904f3f60cff096e6b9b9e3524acd/README.md

Actual implementation and latest full commit message/trailers reviewed. No candidate software executed. Source explicitly omits EAE; author reports CHEKMO-II operation. This is an informal compact emulator, not a fidelity-validated simulator. No explicit license grant found; source contains a copyright attribution only.

## Research event notes

Bounded broad discovery with programmatic URL screening before pinned implementation/build/licence inspection and title/lineage deduplication. Not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Source completeness, rights and AI attribution remain qualified. No upstream software executed; all earlier catalogue records preserved.
